We can also do exact pairwise continuous exchange: Choose two positions i (donor) and j (recipient), adjust mass δ from i to j: h_i -=δ, h_j +=δ, keep sum. Bitmask objective function:
```
def bit_maxc(mask, n):
    best=0
    for d in range(1,n):
        # positive
        x = mask & ~ (mask >> d) & ((1 << (n-d))-1)
        c = x.bit_count()
        if c>best: best=c
        # negative
        y = mask & ~(mask >> d) & (~((1 << d)-1)) ???
```
For negative: low = mask & ~((1<<d)-1) (bits i>=d). c = (low & ~(mask >> d)).bit_count(). But `~` on Python int infinite two's complement; need mask bits within n. Use `alln = (1<<n)-1`. For positive: valid = alln ^ ((1<< (n-d))-1)? Better precomputed `pos_valid[d]=(1<<(n-d))-1`. Then `pos = (mask & ~(mask >> d) & pos_valid[d])`. Since `~(mask>>d)` has infinite ones above n, but & pos_valid restricts. For negative: `neg_valid = alln ^ ((1<<d)-1)` (bits d..n-1). `neg = (mask & ~(mask >> d) & neg_valid)`. Because mask>>d gives h_{i-d} at bit i for i≥d, zeros below; & neg_valid. Good.
Precompute pos_valid list and neg_valid for n. Each eval loop d=1..n-1, two bitcounts.
Could optimize by pos and neg related? c_neg(d) of mask equals c_pos(d) of reversed mask. Max same. We can compute max over positive and negative by also using reversed mask? Could reverse bits once? In eval, do both loops. 498 bitcounts per eval. We can reduce by random? Fine.

Compute A_s arrays: We can compute derivative of c_s wrt h_i and h_j. For each s, formula above. But perhaps easier: A_s = -∂c_s/∂h_i + ∂c_s/∂h_j. We can compute values for all s using small vector operations for a single position p.
For a fixed p, gradient vector over all shifts s can be computed quickly:
For shift s, grad_p(s):
If s>=0:
  as first if p+s<n: 1 - h[p+s]
  as second if p-s>=0: -h[p-s]
for s<0:
  k=-s:
  as first if p-k>=0? Wait earlier for s negative: first p+s=p-k >=0 => 1 - h[p-k]; second p-s=p+k<n => -h[p+k].
So grad_p(s) = (if p+s in [0,n): 1 - h[p+s]) else 0 + (if p-s in [0,n): -h[p-s]) else 0. This formula works for all s, including s=0? For s=0: (1-h[p]) + (-h[p]) = 1-2h[p], correct.
Thus for each p, gradient over s ∈ [-(n-1),n-1] is:
- For s such that 0≤p+s<n: value += 1 - h[p+s]
- For s such that 0≤p-s<n: value += -h[p-s]
These are intervals of s. We can build array Gp length L with slice assignments:
Index idx = s + (n-1).
First interval s1 = -p to n-1-p -> idx from n-1-p to n-1 + (n-1-p)=2n-2-p. Values over q=p+s from 0..n-1: 1 - h[q]. So Gp[idx1:idx2+1] += 1 - h[0..n-1] (full h!). Wait q=p+s, as s ranges full valid, q ranges 0..n-1 exactly. So first contribution is an array `1 - h` centered: for idx from n-1-p to 2n-2-p, add 1-h[:n]. But overlaps with second; that's correct (for s=0 both active).
Second interval s2 = p-n+1 to p -> idx from n-1 + (p-n+1)=p to n-1+p. q=p-s ranges n-1..0? Let q=p-s; as s from p-n+1 to p, q from n-1 down to 0. Value -h[q]. So Gp[idx2:idx2+n] -= h[::-1]? Specifically idx p corresponds s=p-n+1, q=n-1; idx p+n-1 corresponds s=p, q=0. We can add -h[::-1] to slice p:p+n? But slice length n. However L=2n-1, p:n? For p up to n-1, slice p:p+n exceeds L? p+n-1 max 2n-2, start p, length n => end p+n <=2n-1? For p=n-1, end 2n-1, index out by 1 (last 2n-2). Actually indices p .. 2n-2 inclusive length n-1? Let's recalc second valid s: p-s ∈ [0,n-1] => s ∈ [p-(n-1), p]. Number (n) values from p-n+1 to p inclusive. Indices: n-1 + p-n+1 = p; n-1+p = 2n-2+p? Wait index = n-1+s. For s=p, index = n-1+p. If p=n-1, index=n-1+n-1=2n-2 max. For s=p-(n-1), index = n-1+p-n+1 = p. So slice p : n-1+p+1 = p:n+p. Length n. For p=n-1, end=2n-1? n+p=2n-1, index exclusive 2n-1, includes 2n-2, okay L=2n-1 last index 2n-2. End can equal L, fine. For p=0, slice 0:n includes indices 0..n-1, second s from -(n-1) to0, q n-1..0? correct.
First slice: s from -p to n-1-p. Index start=n-1-p, end=n-1 + n-1-p +1 = 2n-1-p. length n. For p=0, start=n-1 end=2n-1 includes n-1..2n-2. Values h[0..n-1]. Good.
So Gp can be built:
```
G = np.zeros(L)
G[n-1-p : 2n-1-p] += 1.0 - h
G[p : n+p] -= h[::-1]
```

Implement pairwise exchange local search efficiently:
- Maintain h (float), c vector (`np.correlate(h,1-h)`) raw length L.
- For candidate pair (i,j), compute A = gradient diff, B as above.
- Need minimize f(δ)=max(c + Aδ + Bδ^2) over interval. But max over all s. Direct golden: define function `value(delta)` returning max of that expression. - However due c0 concavity, golden may not find global min over full interval. But if c0 not max, ignore. We can detect if at the golden best, c0 is among max? We can compute final vals and check. Or use sample: evaluate f at lower, upper, 0, and any point where c0 (concave) could be min? For one concave quadratic plus convex, minimum of max often at an intersection of c0 and another function or at convex minimizer. Pairwise exchange with block coordinate can be a strong optimizer. Let's design algorithm:
```
def pair_polish(h, n, deadline, epochs=..., pairs_per_epoch=50, refine=True, seed=...):
    h = project_feasible
    c = correlate(h,1-h)
    current = c.max()
    for epoch in range(epochs until time):
       best_delta=None; best_pair=None; best_max=current; best_vals=None
       for _ in range(P):
          i,j random distinct with h[i]>1e-9 and h[j]<1-1e-9 (if not skip)
          lower, upper interval
          if upper-lower < 1e-9 continue
          A = grad_diff(i,j,h,n,L)
          B = np.zeros(L); B[n-1]=-2; if d>0 B[...] =1
          # Quick lower bound: maybe f at a few deltas? To avoid full grid for hopeless pairs? Could compute derivative at 0? If max at 0 and derivative doesn't decrease, maybe still curved? We can do small grid 17.
          # Use coarse grid G=33 (full interval) to find min fvals. (including endpoints and 0)
          deltas = np.linspace(lower,upper,G)
          vals = c[None,:] + A[None,:]*deltas[:,None] + B[None,:]*deltas[:,None]**2
          f = vals.max(axis=1)
          idx = argmin(f)
          if f[idx] < best_max:
             # refine around idx with 33 points in neighboring interval, maybe also local golden
             lo = max(lower, deltas[max(0,idx-2)])
             hi = min(upper, deltas[min(G-1, idx+2)])
             deltas2 = np.linspace(lo,hi,33)
             vals2=...
             f2=...
             if f2.min() < best_max: accept candidate with exact c? 
       if best_pair found:
          h[i]-=delta; h[j]+=delta; recompute c = correlate(h,1-h); current=max; update best
       else if multiple epochs no improvement:
          break or increase random perturb
```

```
def grad_diff_pair(h,i,j,n,L):
    p = n-1
    G = np.zeros(L)
    # For p_i: first slice etc. For diff Gj-Gi, do slices:
    # Gj:
    G[p-j : 2*n - j] += 1.0 - h
    G[j : n+j] -= h_rev
    # Gi subtract:
    G[p-i : 2*n - i] -= (1.0 - h)
    G[i : n+i] += h_rev
    return G
```
Need `h_rev = h[::-1]` view. For first slice: `G[n-1-p : 2*n-1-p]`? Earlier first end exclusive 2n-1-p. Let's use start = n-1-p, stop = 2*n-1-p. Length n. For p=0,n=2: start=1 stop=3 (L=3) OK. For p=1: start=0 stop=2. But for first contribution q=p+s; as s ranges -p..n-1-p, q=0..n-1. For p=1,n=2: start=0, stop=2 => idx0,1. Values 1-h[0],1-h[1]. Let's test p=1 s=-1,0? first valid q=p+s: s=-1=>q0 value1-h0 at idx0 (s=-1 idx0); s=0=>q1 value1-h1 idx1. Good.
Second slice start=p, stop=n+p? For p=1,n=2: start=1, stop=3 => idx1,2 values -h[::-1] = [-h1,-h0] to idx1(s=0) and idx2(s=1). For p=1,s=-? second valid s=p-(n-1)=0? Wait p=1,n=2, p-s in [0,1]: s ∈ [0,1]. idx1(s=0): -h1, idx2(s=1): -h0. Correct.

hrev = h[::-1]
# add Gj

G[i:n+i] += hrev
```

def pair_polish(h, n, deadline, max_pairs_total, seed, eps=1e-10, verbose=False):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).astype(float)
    L=2*n-1; p0=n-1
    c = correlate_counts(h,n)  # np.correlate(h,1-h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c=cur
    rng = RandomState(seed)
    one_minus_h = 1.0-h; hrev = h[::-1]
    # For updates, c and one_minus_h/hrev update per accepted move. instead of full recompute for evaluation? We recompute after each accepted move for c, but one_minus/hrev from h.
    no_improve = 0
    evaluated = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # choose batch size adapt
       batch = 1000 maybe
       best_delta=0; bi=bj=None; best_f=cur+1e-12; best_deltas=None
       for _ in range(batch):
          if time up break
          i = rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n); if i==j continue
          lo = max(h[i]-1.0, -h[j]); hi = min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
          if hi - lo < 1e-8: continue
          # compute A
          G = np.zeros(L)
          G[n-1-j : 2*n-1-j] += one_minus_h
          G[j : n+j] -= hrev
          G[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] -= one_minus_h
          G[i : n+i] += hrev
          # Quick derivative maybe? We can evaluate grid.
          # Use coarse grid including >0; maybe uniform 25. If best at endpoints, could be large.
          Ggrid = np.linspace(lo, hi, 25)
          D = Ggrid[:,None]
          vals = c[None,:] + G[None,:]*D
          # add B
          sq = D[:,0]*D[:,0]
          vals[:, p0] += -2.0*sq
          d = j - i
          if d > 0:
             vals[:, p0+d] += sq
             vals[:, p0-d] += sq
          elif d < 0:
             vals[:, p0+d] += sq  # index p0+d where d negative
             vals[:, p0-d] += sq
          f = vals.max(axis=1)
          idx = int(f.argmin())
          if f[idx] < best_f:
             best_f = float(f[idx]); best_delta = float(Ggrid[idx]); bi=i; bj=j; best_deltas=Ggrid
       if best_f < cur - 1e-10:
          # refine accepted pair
          lo2 = max(best_deltas[max(0, idx-3)], lower?) We didn't store lower/upper? store
          # refine with 51 points in neighborhood plus maybe full if best_f not much? Use local.
          h[bi] -= best_delta? Need final delta after refine.
          ...
          recompute c, update one_minus/hrev, cur
          no_improve=0
          update global best if c.max()<global
       else:
          no_improve += 1
          if no_improve > 10: perturb h small, recompute c, best_f=cur; no_improve=0
    return best
```

List of helper functions:
- `project_feasible(h, n)` (water filling projection). Use binary search or sort? For n small, Newton? We'll implement robust binary search for λ.
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if abs(h.sum()-target)<1e-12: return h
    lo = target - n  # max negative shift? Actually h+λ, λ ∈ [target - n, target]
    hi = target
    # We need sum clip(v+λ) monotonic. find λ where sum=target.
    for _ in range(60): mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum(); if s<target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # correct tiny residual by adding to variables with max room? 
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       room=1-h
       if room.sum()>0: h += diff/room.sum()*room; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    elif diff<0:
       s=h.sum()
       if s>0: h += diff/s * h; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This projection may not be exact Euclidean but fine. Could use sort waterfill for exact; binary search 60*250 trivial. After correction, sum should be close. We can do a loop few times.

- `compute_c5(h,n)`: `dx=2.0/n; c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); return float(c.max()*dx)`, and maybe return c. We'll use `correlate_counts(h)` function.
```
def correlate_counts(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full').astype(float)
def c5_from_counts(c,n): return float(np.max(c)*2.0/n)
```

Within pair_polish:
```
def pair_polish(h, n, deadline, seed=42, max_batch=500, patience=25):
    h = project_feasible(h,n).astype(float)
    L=2*n-1; p0=n-1
    c = correlate_counts(h,n)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = cur
    one_minus = 1.0 - h; hrev = h[::-1]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # active indices for direction condition: c >= cur - 1e-7? use 2e-6
    # We need update active after each accept.
    no_improve = 0
    sigma = 0.15
    Coarse=49; Refine=73
    t = time.time()
    while time.time() < deadline:
        active = np.where(c >= cur - 1e-7)[0]
        active_nz = active[active != p0]
        # Batch loop maybe until 0.3s or batch size
        best_f = cur
        best_i = best_j = None; best_lo=best_hi=best_delta=0
        started = time.time()
        # batch size adapt: stop if time > 0.4s
        while time.time() - started < 0.35 and time.time() < deadline:
            i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
            if i==j: continue
            hi = min(h[i], 1.0-h[j]); lo = max(h[i]-1.0, -h[j])
            if hi - lo < 1e-8: continue
            A = grad_diff_pair(h,i,j,n,L)
            pos_ok = False; neg_ok = False
            if p0 !=? active_nz:
                if len(active_nz)==0 or A[active_nz].max() <= 1e-7: pos_ok = hi > 1e-9
                if len(active_nz)==0 or A[active_nz].min() >= -1e-7: neg_ok = lo < -1e-9
            else: ...
            # Actually active_nz may include? 
            if not (pos_ok or neg_ok): continue
            if pos_ok: check interval [0,hi]; if hi>0
            if neg_ok: check [lo,0]
            # For one interval, call pair_eval_f; if fmin<best_f store.
        if best_i is not None and best_f < cur - 1e-9:
            # refine
            A = grad_diff_pair(h,best_i,best_j,n,L)
            delta = refine_pair_delta(c,A,best_i,best_j,n,best_lo,best_hi,best_delta)
            # verify
            fval, deltas = pair_eval_f(c,A,best_i,best_j,n,np.array([delta]))
            # Actually delta from refine already min; check fval<cur
            if fval[0] < cur - 1e-10:
                h[best_i] -= delta; h[best_j] += delta
                h = project_feasible(h,n)
                one_minus = 1.0 - h; hrev = h[::-1]
                c = correlate_counts(h,n); cur = float(c.max())
                if cur < best_c:
                    best_c = cur; best_h = h.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                sigma = min(0.2, sigma*1.0) # maybe
            else:
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if no_improve >= patience:
            # perturb from best_h
            h = best_h.copy()
            h = h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
            h = project_feasible(h,n)
            one_minus=1.0-h; hrev=h[::-1]
            c=correlate_counts(h,n); cur=float(c.max())
            # maybe if perturbed worse than best? keep current local; no_improve=0
            no_improve = 0
            sigma = max(0.02, sigma*0.85)
    return best_h, best_c
```

`make_bit_masks(n)` returns list pos, neg.
```
def make_bit_masks(n):
    alln=(1<<n)-1
    pos=[]; neg=[]
    for d in range(1,n):
       pos.append((1 << (n-d)) - 1)
       neg.append(alln ^ ((1 << d) - 1))
    return pos,neg
```
`mask_from_h(h,n)`: loop.
```
def h_to_mask(h,n):
    m=0
    for i in range(n):
       if h[i] >= 0.5: m |= 1<<i
    return m
```
Binarize continuous top half exactly n/2:
```
def top_half_binary(h,n):
    order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    hbin=np.zeros(n)
    hbin[order[:n//2]]=1
    return hbin
```
But for mask, use top half.

`bit_score`:
```
def bit_score(mask,n,posv,negv):
    maxc=0; cnt=0; sumsq=0
    for d in range(1,n):
       c=(mask & ~(mask>>d) & posv[d-1]).bit_count()
       if c>maxc: maxc=c; cnt=1
       elif c==maxc: cnt+=1
       sumsq += c*c
       c=(mask & ~(mask>>d) & negv[d-1]).bit_count()
       update
    return maxc,cnt,sumsq
```
Total loops 249*2. Each bit_count. For 200k evals maybe 100M bit_counts; could be heavy. Maybe limit binary search to ~20-40k evals. But can run 150s? Let's estimate bit_score 498 bit_count loops in Python. Big-int bit_count of 250 bits is extremely fast ~20ns? loop overhead ~50ns? Actually Python loop ~50-100ns? More like 50 ns for simple? 500 loops*100ns=50us. 100k=5s. Maybe okay 500k=25s. We'll set binary time 120s; let it run until deadline.

Pair functions:
```
def grad_diff_pair(h,i,j,n,L):
    p0=n-1
    G=np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    omh=1.0 - h
    hrev=h[::-1]
    G[p0-j:2*n-1-j] += omh
    G[j:n+j] -= hrev
    G[p0-i:2*n-1-i] -= omh
    G[i:n+i] += hrev
    return G
```
Check slice `2*n-1-j`: for j=0, start=n-1, stop=2n-1 = L, ok. For j=n-1, stop=n, start=0, ok.

`pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas)`:
```
def pair_eval_f(c,A,i,j,n,deltas):
    L=len(c); p0=n-1
    D=deltas[:,None]
    vals=c[None,:] + A[None,:]*D
    sq=(D[:,0]*D[:,0])[:,None]  # shape G,1 to add columns?
    vals[:,p0] += -2.0 * D[:,0]*D[:,0]
    d=j-i
    if d != 0:
       vals[:,p0+d] += D[:,0]*D[:,0]
       vals[:,p0-d] += D[:,0]*D[:,0]
    return vals.max(axis=1)
```
Use `sq1 = D[:,0]*D[:,0]`.
If d negative, p0+d and p0-d are valid distinct. If d=0 skip.

`pair_find_best(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi)`:

```
def pair_search_interval(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi,G):
    if hi - lo < 1e-10: return None
    deltas=np.linspace(lo,hi,G)
    f=pair_eval_f(...)
    idx=int(f.argmin())
    return float(f[idx]), float(deltas[idx])
```
`refine_pair(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi,center)`:
```
def refine_pair_delta(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi,center):
    span=hi-lo
    w=max(1e-8, span/20.0)
    a=max(lo, center-2.0*w)
    b=min(hi, center+2.0*w)
    if b-a < 1e-10:
       if center < hi: a=max(lo, hi-1.0e-5); b=hi
       else: a=lo; b=min(hi, lo+1.0e-5)
    # include 0 if inside? but interval one-sided includes endpoint; full could min near0. Since center from coarse maybe 0 if full and best 0 (not improvement). If full and best near0, local includes.
    deltas=np.linspace(a,b,81)
    f=pair_eval_f(...)
    return float(deltas[int(f.argmin())])
```

```
def pair_polish(h, n, stop, seed=42):
    start = time.time()
    h = project_feasible(h.astype(float), n)
    c = compute_counts(h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_raw=cur
    one_minus=1-h; hrev=h[::-1]
    p0=n-1
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_imp=0; sigma=0.12
    coarse=57; refine_pts=81
    while time.time() < stop:
        # If too close stop, break
        active = np.nonzero(c >= cur - 1e-8)[0]
        anz = active[active != p0]
        an_avg = A[anz] if anz.size else None
        best_f=cur; bi=bj=None; blo=bhi=bdelta=0.0
        batch_start=time.time()
        # optional dynamic pairs until 0.25s
        while time.time() - batch_start < 0.25 and time.time() < stop:
             i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
             if i==j: continue
             hi=interval...
             lo=...
             if hi-lo < 1e-9: continue
             A=grad_diff_pair(h,i,j,n,2*n-1)
             # direction
             if anz.size:
                av=A[anz]
                zero=np.abs(av) <= 1e-7
                darr=j-i
                quad=(anz == p0+darr) | (anz == p0-darr)
                bad_pos = np.any(av > 1e-7) or np.any(zero & quad)
                bad_neg = np.any(av < -1e-7) or np.any(zero & quad)
             else: bad_pos=bad_neg=False
             if hi > 1e-9 and not bad_pos: test [0,hi]
             if lo < -1e-9 and not bad_neg: test [lo,0]
             if both bad? If anz empty, both tests? To avoid duplicate for full interval, if both allowed maybe choose full [lo,hi] instead of two. But if both allowed because anz empty or all A=0/no quad. If we do [lo,hi] with one grid, okay. But direction conditions: if posi ok and negi ok. We can do:
             allowed=0
             if hi>eps and not bad_pos: allowed|=1
             if lo<-eps and not bad_neg: allowed|=2
             if allowed==3: lo2,hi2=lo,hi
             elif allowed==1: 0,hi
             elif allowed==2: lo,0
             else continue
             fval,delta=pair_search_interval(...)
             if fval < best_f - 1e-12: store
        if bi is not None and best_f < cur - 1e-10:
             # refine
             A=grad_diff_pair(...); delta=refine_pair_delta(...)
             # verify with fpair at delta
             fval=float(pair_eval_f(c,A,bi,bj,n,np.array([delta]))[0])
             if fval < cur - 1e-10:
                 h[bi]-=delta; h[bj]+=delta
                 h=project_feasible(h,n)
                 one_minus=...; hrev=...

                 if cur < best_raw:

                 no_imp=0
             else: no_imp+=1
        else:
             no_imp+=1
        if no_imp >= 20:

             h = best_h.astype(float).copy()
             # Add noise
             h = h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
             h = project_feasible(h,n)
             one_minus=1-h; hrev=h[::-1]
             c=compute_counts(h); cur=float(c.max())
             no_imp=0
             sigma = max(0.015, sigma*0.85)
             # optional: if cur > best_raw + 0.5, keep current? We can reset h to current local maybe? But perturb from best.
    return best_h, best_raw
```

Now binary SA code.

```
def bit_score(mask,n,posv,negv):
    maxc=0; cnt=0; ssum=0
    for k in range(n-1):
       d=k+1
       c=(mask & ~(mask>>d) & posv[k]).bit_count()
       if c > maxc: maxc=c; cnt=1
       elif c == maxc: cnt += 1
       ssum += c*c
       c=(mask & ~(mask>>d) & negv[k]).bit_count()
       update
    return maxc,cnt,ssum
```
If n=1? n≥? no.

`energy_bit(score)`: `maxc*100.0 + cnt*10.0 + ssum/1000.0`. But sumsq can be large (around 500*31^2=480k); /1000=480, bigger than primary? primary maxc 50*100=5000; okay. Secondary sumsq difference maybe tens. Use /10000 =>48.

Binary SA:
```
def binary_sa(mask,n,stop,seed,posv,negv):
    rng=RandomState(seed)
    cur=bit_score(...)
    E=energy(cur)
    best=cur; best_mask=mask; best_E=E
    T=80.0; T_end=1.0
    total_est? We can just while time<stop:
       # generate move
       while True: i=rand; if not bit: break
       while True: j=rand; if bit: break
       new=mask ^ (1<<i) ^ (1<<j)
       ns=bit_score(new)
       nE=energy
       if nE < E or (T>1e-3 and rng.random() < math.exp(-(nE-E)/T)):
           mask=new; cur=ns; E=nE
           if E < best_E - 1e-9: best=...
       # cool
       T = max(T_end, T*0.9999)

Binarization from continuous: `top_half_binary` function:
```
def top_half_binary(h,n):
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    out=np.zeros(n)
    out[idx[:n//2]]=1.0
    return out
```

Random balanced mask:
```
def random_balanced_mask(n, rng):
    arr = np.concatenate((np.ones(n//2), np.zeros(n//2)))
    rng.shuffle(arr)
    mask=0
    for i,v in enumerate(arr): if v: mask |= 1<<i
    return mask
```
For n=250, loop.

Convert mask to h:
```
def mask_to_h(mask,n):
    h=np.zeros(n)
    m=mask
    # loop i 0..n-1
    for i in range(n):
       if (m>>i)&1: h[i]=1.0
    return h
```

Now, `fill_grad_diff` uses `omh=1.0 - h` and `hrev=h[::-1]` each call. We can use existing `one_minus` and `hrev` buffers from pair_polish to avoid recomputing? But h changes; one_minus is array 1-h; hrev view. We can pass them:
```
def fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hrev, i,j,n):
   A[:] = 0.0
   p0=n-1
   A[p0-j:2*n-1-j] += omh
   A[j:n+j] -= hrev
   A[p0-i:2*n-1-i] -= omh
   A[i:n+i] += hrev
```
In pair_polish, maintain `omh = 1.0 - h`, `hrev = h[::-1]`. After h changes, reassign. This saves. Good.

Let's write functions:
```
def pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas):
    p0 = n - 1
    D = deltas
    sq = D * D
    vals = c[None, :] + A[None, :] * D[:, None]
    vals[:, p0] -= 2.0 * sq
    d = j - i
    if d:
        vals[:, p0 + d] += sq
        vals[:, p0 - d] += sq
    return vals.max(axis=1)
```
Need ensure `p0+d` within 0..L-1; d between -(n-1), n-1. Good.

`pair_search_interval`:
```
def pair_search_interval(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi,G):
    if hi <= lo: return np.inf, lo
    deltas=np.linspace(lo,hi,G)
    f=pair_eval_f(c,A,i,j,n,deltas)
    idx=int(f.argmin())
    return float(f[idx]), float(deltas[idx])
```

`refine_pair_delta`:
```
def refine_pair_delta(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi,center):
    span=hi-lo
    w=max(1e-8, span/25.0)
    a=center-2.0*w; b=center+2.0*w
    if a<lo: a=lo
    if b>hi: b=hi
    if b-a < 1e-8:
        if center < hi - 1e-12:
            a=max(lo, hi-0.4*span); b=hi
        else:
            a=lo; b=min(hi, lo+0.4*span)
    deltas=np.linspace(a,b,81)
    f=pair_eval_f(...)
    return float(deltas[int(f.argmin())])
```
If center at lo and span small, a=lo,b=lo+0.4span. If center at hi, a=hi-0.4span,b=hi. Good.

Now, pair_polish inner search: For allowed interval, if full (allowed==3), lo2=lo hi2=hi. But if full interval with hi/lo large, coarse G=57. If min near 0, local refine width span/25 ~0.04 if span1; 81 points step0.0005; good.

Detailed functions:

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(2):
        lo = target - n
        hi = target
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
            if s < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot > 0:
                h += (diff / tot) * room
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot > 0:
                h += (diff / tot) * h
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        if (1.0 - h[idx]) >= diff:
            h[idx] += diff
    elif diff < 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(h))
        if h[idx] >= -diff:
            h[idx] += diff
    return h
```
The final single variable adjust could break box if residual > max room; but residual tiny. Good. If residual larger, loop may not fix. Maybe we should not single adjust if larger to avoid box; but target 1e-12. okay.

`compute_counts`:
```
def compute_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')[::-1]
```
Maybe `.astype(float, copy=False)`. If h int, returns int; pair_eval needs float. Use `astype(float)`.
```
def compute_counts(h):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return c[::-1].astype(float, copy=False)
```
`copy=False` with astype may copy if needed.

`fill_grad_diff`:
```
def fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hrev, i, j, n):
    A[:] = 0.0
    p = n - 1
    A[p - j:2 * n - 1 - j] += omh
    A[j:n + j] -= hrev
    A[p - i:2 * n - 1 - i] -= omh
    A[i:n + i] += hrev
```
Check slice `n + j` exclusive. For j=n-1, end=2n-1=L ok. `2*n-1-j` exclusive. For j=0, start=p, end=2n-1=L. Good.

`pair_eval_f`:
```
def pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas):
    p = n - 1
    D = deltas.astype(float, copy=False)
    sq = D * D
    vals = c[None, :] + A[None, :] * D[:, None]
    vals[:, p] -= 2.0 * sq
    d = j - i
    if d:
        vals[:, p + d] += sq
        vals[:, p - d] += sq
    return vals.max(axis=1)
```
If `deltas` list? we pass array. `astype` okay.

`pair_search_interval`:
```
def pair_search_interval(c, A, i, j, n, lo, hi, pts):
    if hi - lo < 1e-10:
        return float(c.max()), lo
    deltas = np.linspace(lo, hi, pts)
    f = pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas)
    idx = int(f.argmin())
    return float(f[idx]), float(deltas[idx])
```
`c.max()` maybe not needed.

`refine_pair_delta`:
```
def refine_pair_delta(c,A,i,j,n,lo,hi,center):
    span = hi - lo
    w = max(1e-8, span / 25.0)
    a = center - 2.0 * w
    b = center + 2.0 * w
    if a < lo: a = lo
    if b > hi: b = hi
    if b - a < 1e-8:
        if center < hi - 1e-12:
            a = max(lo, hi - max(1e-6, 0.25 * span))
            b = hi
        else:
            a = lo
            b = min(hi, lo + max(1e-6, 0.25 * span))
    deltas = np.linspace(a, b, 81)
    f = pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas)
    return float(deltas[int(f.argmin())])
```

`pair_polish`: Let's write robust and compact.
```
def pair_polish(h, n, stop, seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(h.astype(float, copy=True), n)
    c = compute_counts(h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_r = cur
    p = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    hrev = h[::-1]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    no_imp = 0
    sigma = 0.12
    pts = 57
    while time.time() < stop:
        active = np.nonzero(c >= cur - 1e-8)[0]
        anz = active[active != p]
        has_anz = anz.size > 0
        best_f = cur
        bi = bj = -1
        blo = bhi = bd = 0.0
        t0b = time.time()
        while time.time() - t0b < 0.22 and time.time() < stop:
            i = rng.randint(n); j = rng.randint(n)
            if i == j: continue
            lo = max(h[i] - 1.0, -h[j])
            hi = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
            if hi - lo < 1e-9: continue
            fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hrev, i, j, n)
            d = j - i
            if has_anz:
                av = A[anz]
                zero = np.abs(av) <= 1e-7
                quad = (anz == p + d) | (anz == p - d)
                bad_pos = np.any(av > 1e-7) or np.any(zero & quad)
                bad_neg = np.any(av < -1e-7) or np.any(zero & quad)
            else:
                bad_pos = False; bad_neg = False
            int_lo = int_hi = None
            if hi > 1e-9 and lo < -1e-9 and not bad_pos and not bad_neg:
                int_lo, int_hi = lo, hi
            elif hi > 1e-9 and not bad_pos:
                int_lo, int_hi = 0.0, hi
            elif lo < -1e-9 and not bad_neg:
                int_lo, int_hi = lo, 0.0
            else:
                continue
            fval, dval = pair_search_interval(c, A, i, j, n, int_lo, int_hi, pts)
            if fval < best_f - 1e-12:
                best_f = fval; bi=i; bj=j; blo=int_lo; bhi=int_hi; bd=dval
        if bi >= 0 and best_f < cur - 1e-10:
            fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hrev, bi, bj, n)
            delta = refine_pair_delta(c, A, bi, bj, n, blo, bhi, bd)
            fval = float(pair_eval_f(c, A, bi, bj, n, np.array([delta]))[0])
            old = cur
            if fval < old - 1e-10:
                h[bi] -= delta
                h[bj] += delta
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = compute_counts(h)
                cur = float(c.max())
                omh = 1.0 - h
                hrev = h[::-1]
                if cur < old - 1e-12:
                    no_imp = 0
                    if cur < best_r:
                        best_r = cur; best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
            else:
                no_imp += 1
        else:
            no_imp += 1
        if no_imp >= 30:
            h = best_h.copy()
            h = h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n)
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = compute_counts(h)
            cur = float(c.max())
            omh = 1.0 - h
            hrev = h[::-1]
            no_imp = 0
            sigma = max(0.02, sigma * 0.85)
    return best_h, best_r
```
Potential bug: When `has_anz` and `d` = 0? i==j skipped, d nonzero. `quad` boolean arrays. If `anz` length large, `np.any` okay.
Active set within 1e-8; if only c0 active and c0 not at max? no.
If active_nz empty and lo/hi both sides, int full. Good.
If bad_pos false but hi>eps, bad_neg false but lo<eps? full. If full, we don't enforce that pos/neg both allowed? yes both not bad.
What if active_nz has A values both positive and negative but c0 active? bad both true, skip. Could a finite move reduce max by simultaneously changing active positive and negative? If there is an active nonc0 with A>0, positive direction impossible; if another with A<0, negative impossible. No direction possible. skip correct.

Now, `pair_search_interval` uses coarse `pts=57`. If interval full length 1, step ~0.018. okay.

If pair_polish called with stop in past, returns initial.

Now binary functions. `make_bit_masks` returns lists; for n=250, okay. If n large 1000, lists of big ints. Fine.

`bit_score` can be optimized by local variables. Use `pos = posv; neg = negv`.
```
def bit_score(mask,n,posv,negv):
    maxc=0; cnt=0; ssq=0
    for d in range(1,n):
       c=(mask & ~(mask >> d) & posv[d-1]).bit_count()
       if c>maxc: maxc=c; cnt=1
       elif c==maxc: cnt+=1
       ssq += c*c
       c=(mask & ~(mask >> d) & negv[d-1]).bit_count()
       if c>maxc: maxc=c; cnt=1
       elif c==maxc: cnt+=1
       ssq += c*c
    return maxc,cnt,ssq
```
`~(mask>>d)` infinite but & valid restricts; neg valid already restricted and includes only bits where high enough. For positive, `posv` valid bits. Good.

`bit_energy(score)` at top:
```
def bit_energy(sc):
    return 100.0*sc[0] + 10.0*sc[1] + 0.001*sc[2]
```
Maybe sumsq/1000. For maxc 47, term=4700; cnt~10 term=100; ssq maybe 500*40^2=800k *0.001=800. Good.

`h_to_mask`:
```
def h_to_mask(h,n):
    m=0
    for i in range(n):
        if h[i] >= 0.5:
            m |= (1 << i)
    return m
```
`mask_to_h`:
```
def mask_to_h(mask,n):
    h=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h
```
`top_half_binary`:
```
def top_half_binary(h,n):
    out=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    out[idx[:n//2]]=1.0
    return out
```
`random_balanced_mask`:
```
def random_balanced_mask(n,rng):
    arr=np.ones(n//2, dtype=float)
    arr=np.concatenate((arr, np.zeros(n//2, dtype=float)))
    rng.shuffle(arr)
    return h_to_mask(arr,n)
```
This concatenates, no huge literal.

`binary_sa`:
```
def binary_sa(mask,n,stop,seed,posv,negv):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    cur=bit_score(mask,n,posv,negv)
    E=bit_energy(cur)
    best=cur; best_mask=mask; best_E=E
    t0=time.time()
    T=80.0; Tend=1.0
    stag=0
    while time.time() < stop:
       # choose zero
       while True:
           i=rng.randint(n)
           if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0: break
       while True:
           j=rng.randint(n)
           if ((mask >> j) & 1) == 1: break
       new = mask ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
       sc = bit_score(new,n,posv,negv)
       nE = bit_energy(sc)
       accept=False
       if nE < E - 1e-9:
           accept=True
       elif T > 1e-3:
           deltaE = nE - E
           if deltaE < 0 or rng.random() < math.exp(-deltaE / T):
               accept=True
       if accept:
           mask=new; cur=sc; E=nE
           if E < best_E - 1e-9:
               best_E=E; best=cur; best_mask=mask; stag=0
           else:
               stag += 1
       else:
           stag += 1
       frac = 1.0
       den = stop - t0
       if den > 0:
           frac = max(0.0, 1.0 - (time.time() - t0) / den)
       T = Tend + (T - Tend) * frac  # linear cool
       if stag > 8000:
           # random triple swap
           mask = mask ^ (1 << rng.randint(n)) ? Need ensure zero/one? Could just reinit? 
           cur=bit_score(...); E=...
           T = max(T, 30.0)
           stag=0
    return best_mask, best
```
In random triple, simpler: if stagnation, create new mask by swapping 3 random zero-one pairs from current. Need while. Could just reset current with a random balanced mask? But may lose best; we keep best. Use `new = random_balanced_mask(n, rng)`? That resets far. Maybe too disruptive. Use several swaps:
```
for _ in range(3):
  while zero...; while one...; new ^=...
mask=new; cur=bit_score...
```
But if stationary, maybe. Keep code.

Cooling linear: `Tend + (Tstart - Tend)*frac`, Tstart 80. At start frac1 T=80, end T=1. Good. `stag` resets on best improvement, not every improvement. Maybe reset on accept? no. If no best for 8000, restart. 8000 moves maybe few seconds.

`binary_sa` may be heavy; ensure stop. `frac` each iteration time call overhead small.

Potential issue: `math.exp(-deltaE/T)` with deltaE large positive e.g. 1000, exp underflows 0; okay. If deltaE negative, we already accept. T >1e-3.

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    dl=t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - 10.0)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 250
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_raw = float(compute_counts(h0).max())
    total = dl - t0
    # Phase 1
    if total > 120:
        stop1 = t0 + max(60.0, 0.20 * total)
        h1, r1 = pair_polish(h0, n, stop1, seed)
        if r1 < best_raw:
            best_raw, best_h = r1, h1
    # Phase 2 binary
    if n < 500 and time.time() < dl - 150:
        stop2 = t0 + 0.40 * total
        if stop2 < time.time(): stop2 = time.time()
        hbin = top_half_binary(best_h, n)
        start_mask = h_to_mask(hbin, n)
        # maybe include random start? Can't with one function unless time.
        posv, negv = make_bit_masks(n)
        bmask, bscore = binary_sa(start_mask, n, stop2, seed + 1, posv, negv)
        # keep binary candidate
        bin_h = mask_to_h(bmask, n)
        bin_raw = float(bscore[0])
        # phase 3 binary polish if time
        if time.time() < dl - 150:
            stop3 = t0 + 0.65 * total
            hb, rb = pair_polish(bin_h, n, stop3, seed + 2)
            if rb < best_raw:
                best_raw, best_h = rb, hb
            elif rb < best_raw + 1.0: # if close, maybe keep? final from best? We could set a secondary start
                bin_h = hb
        else:
            bin_h = mask_to_h(bmask,n)
    # Final
    if time.time() < dl - 5:
        # choose start: best_h; if binary candidate close and different? maybe alternate two segments
        # If binary candidate from phase3 exists and is close, run half time from it, half from best?
        # Simpler: final from best_h. But we can if bin_h defined and compute_counts(bin_h).max() < best_raw*1.01 and not same, do two-fifths from bin_h?
```

Code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(3):
        lo = target - n
        hi = target
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
            if s < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot > 0:
                h += (diff / tot) * room
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot > 0:
                h += (diff / tot) * h
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        if (1.0 - h[idx]) >= diff:
            h[idx] += diff
    elif diff < 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(h))
        if h[idx] >= -diff:
            h[idx] += diff
    return h
```
~30 lines.

Let's draft pair_polish compact:

```
def pair_polish(h, n, stop, seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(h.astype(float, copy=True), n)
    c = compute_counts(h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_r = cur
    p = n - 1
    A = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    hr = h[::-1]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    pts = 41 if n > 400 else 57
    no = 0
    sig = 0.12
    while time.time() < stop:
        act = np.nonzero(c >= cur - 1e-8)[0]
        nz = act[act != p]
        has = nz.size > 0
        bf = cur
        bi = bj = -1
        blo = bhi = bd = 0.0
        tb = time.time()
        while time.time() - tb < 0.22 and time.time() < stop:
            i = rng.randint(n)
            j = rng.randint(n)
            if i == j:
                continue
            lo = max(h[i] - 1.0, -h[j])
            hi = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
            if hi - lo < 1e-9:
                continue
            fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hr, i, j, n)
            d = j - i
            if has:
                av = A[nz]
                z = np.abs(av) <= 1e-7
                q = (nz == p + d) | (nz == p - d)
                bp = bool(np.any(av > 1e-7) or np.any(z & q))
                bn = bool(np.any(av < -1e-7) or np.any(z & q))
            else:
                bp = bn = False
            if hi > 1e-9 and lo < -1e-9 and not bp and not bn:
                a, b = lo, hi
            elif hi > 1e-9 and not bp:
                a, b = 0.0, hi
            elif lo < -1e-9 and not bn:
                a, b = lo, 0.0
            else:
                continue
            fv, dv = pair_search_interval(c, A, i, j, n, a, b, pts)
            if fv < bf - 1e-12:
                bf = fv; bi = i; bj = j; blo = a; bhi = b; bd = dv
        if bi >= 0 and bf < cur - 1e-10:
            fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hr, bi, bj, n)
            dl = refine_pair_delta(c, A, bi, bj, n, blo, bhi, bd)
            fv = float(pair_eval_f(c, A, bi, bj, n, np.array([dl]))[0])
            if fv < cur - 1e-10:
                old = cur
                h[bi] -= dl
                h[bj] += dl
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = compute_counts(h)
                cur = float(c.max())
                omh = 1.0 - h
                hr = h[::-1]
                if cur < old - 1e-12:
                    no = 0
                    if cur < best_r:
                        best_r = cur
                        best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    no += 1
            else:
                no += 1
        else:
            no += 1
        if no >= 30:
            h = best_h.copy()
            h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n), n)
            c = compute_counts(h)
            cur = float(c.max())
            omh = 1.0 - h
            hr = h[::-1]
            no = 0
            sig = max(0.02, sig * 0.85)
    return best_h, best_r
```
Looks good. Note variable `dl` shadows deadline? local.

        n = len(h0)

        h0 = random_balanced_h(n, rng0) # need function? Could use top_half random mask.

```
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_r = float(compute_counts(h0).max())
    total = max(1.0, dl - t0)
    bin_h = None
    if total > 120:
        stop1 = t0 + max(60.0, 0.20 * total)
        h1, r1 = pair_polish(h0, n, stop1, seed)
        if r1 < best_r:
            best_r, best_h = r1, h1
    if (n % 2 == 0) and n < 600 and time.time() < dl - 120:
        stop2 = t0 + 0.40 * total
        if stop2 < time.time():
            stop2 = time.time()
        hbin = top_half_binary(best_h, n)
        m = h_to_mask(hbin, n)
        pos, neg = make_bit_masks(n)
        bm, sc = binary_sa(m, n, stop2, seed + 1, pos, neg)
        bin_h = mask_to_h(bm, n)
        if time.time() < dl - 120:
            stop3 = t0 + 0.65 * total
            hb, rb = pair_polish(bin_h, n, stop3, seed + 2)
            if rb < best_r:
                best_r, best_h = rb, hb
            if hb is not None:
                bin_h = hb
    starts = [best_h]
    if bin_h is not None:
        br = float(compute_counts(bin_h).max())
        if br < best_r + 2.0:
            starts.append(bin_h)
    end = dl - 3.0
    for idx, sh in enumerate(starts):
        remaining = end - time.time()
        if remaining < 30:
            break
        seg_stop = time.time() + (0.55 * remaining if len(starts) > 1 and idx == 0 else remaining)
        hf, rf = pair_polish(sh, n, seg_stop, seed + 20 + idx)
        if rf < best_r:
            best_r, best_h = rf, hf
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_r = float(compute_counts(best_h).max())
    return best_h, best_r * 2.0 / float(n), n
```

`make_bit_masks`:
```
def make_bit_masks(n):
    allm = (1 << n) - 1
    pv = []
    nv = []
    for d in range(1, n):
        pv.append((1 << (n - d)) - 1)
        nv.append(allm ^ ((1 << d) - 1))
    return pv, nv
```

`bit_score` as above.

`bit_energy` top:
```
def bit_energy(s):
    return 100.0 * s[0] + 10.0 * s[1] + 0.001 * s[2]
```

`random_balanced_mask`:
```
def random_balanced_mask(n, rng):
    v = np.ones(n // 2)
    v = np.concatenate((v, np.zeros(n - n // 2)))
    rng.shuffle(v)
    return h_to_mask(v, n)
```
For even n, n-n//2 = n//2. If odd, zeros one more, but we skip binary if odd; fallback n even. Could sum for n odd? We require top n/2? But random balanced with n odd sum n//2 not n/2? np.ones(n//2), zeros(n-n//2) sum n//2. For n=250 okay. For fallback if odd? n=250.

`binary_sa`:
```
def binary_sa(mask, n, stop, seed, pos, neg):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    cur = bit_score(mask, n, pos, neg)
    E = bit_energy(cur)
    bcur = cur
    bm = mask
    bE = E
    t0 = time.time()
    T0 = 80.0
    Te = 1.0
    stag = 0
    den = max(1.0, stop - t0)
    while time.time() < stop:
        while True:
            i = rng.randint(n)
            if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0:
                break
        while True:
            j = rng.randint(n)
            if ((mask >> j) & 1) != 0:
                break
        nm = mask ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
        nc = bit_score(nm, n, pos, neg)
        nE = bit_energy(nc)
        acc = nE < E - 1e-9
        if not acc:
            frac = max(0.0, 1.0 - (time.time() - t0) / den)
            T = Te + (T0 - Te) * frac
            if T > 1e-3 and nE >= E:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-(nE - E) / T):
                    acc = True
        if acc:
            mask = nm
            cur = nc
            E = nE
            stag = 0
            if E < bE - 1e-9:
                bE = E
                bcur = cur
                bm = mask
        else:
            stag += 1
        if stag > 6000:
            for _ in range(3):
                while True:
                    i = rng.randint(n)
                    if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0:
                        break
                while True:
                    j = rng.randint(n)
                    if ((mask >> j) & 1) != 0:
                        break
                mask ^= (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
            cur = bit_score(mask, n, pos, neg)
            E = bit_energy(cur)
            stag = 0
    return bm, bcur
```
If accept worse, we update current but not best. Good. If T should be computed even if nE<E? We only need for rejection. cooling continues. We compute T only in reject branch; if many accepts, T not used? It will be computed next reject. okay.

Could `math.exp` with large negative underflow to 0; no issue. If `nE - E` negative (but not acc due not < -1e-9? If slightly negative > -1e-9? treat as tie; delta negative in reject branch? We only go to reject if not acc, could be delta -1e-10; `math.exp(positive tiny)` >1, rng random < >1 always accept. okay.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(3):
        lo = target - n
        hi = target
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
            if s < target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot > 0:
                h += (diff / tot) * room
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            if tot > 0:
                h += (diff / tot) * h
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        if (1.0 - h[idx]) >= diff:
            h[idx] += diff
    elif diff < 0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(h))
        if h[idx] >= -diff:
            h[idx] += diff
    return h


def compute_counts(h):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return c[::-1].astype(float, copy=False)


def fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hr, i, j, n):
    A[:] = 0.0
    p = n - 1
    A[p - j:2 * n - 1 - j] += omh
    A[j:n + j] -= hr
    A[p - i:2 * n - 1 - i] -= omh
    A[i:n + i] += hr


def pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas):
    p = n - 1
    D = np.asarray(deltas, dtype=float)
    sq = D * D
    vals = c[None, :] + A[None, :] * D[:, None]
    vals[:, p] -= 2.0 * sq
    d = j - i
    if d:
        vals[:, p + d] += sq
        vals[:, p - d] += sq
    return vals.max(axis=1)


def pair_search_interval(c, A, i, j, n, lo, hi, pts):
    if hi - lo < 1e-10:
        return float(c.max()), lo
    deltas = np.linspace(lo, hi, pts)
    f = pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas)
    idx = int(f.argmin())
    return float(f[idx]), float(deltas[idx])


def refine_pair_delta(c, A, i, j, n, lo, hi, center):
    span = hi - lo
    w = max(1e-8, span / 25.0)
    a = center - 2.0 * w
    b = center + 2.0 * w
    if a < lo:
        a = lo
    if b > hi:
        b = hi
    if b - a < 1e-8:
        if center < hi - 1e-12:
            a = max(lo, hi - max(1e-6, 0.25 * span))
            b = hi
        else:
            a = lo
            b = min(hi, lo + max(1e-6, 0.25 * span))
    deltas = np.linspace(a, b, 81)
    f = pair_eval_f(c, A, i, j, n, deltas)
    return float(deltas[int(f.argmin())])


def pair_polish(h, n, stop, seed=42):
    h = project_feasible(h.astype(float, copy=True), n)
    target = 0.5 * n
    c = compute_counts(h)
    cur = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_r = cur
    p = n - 1
    A = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    hr = h[::-1]
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    pts = 41 if n > 400 else 57
    no = 0
    sig = 0.12
    epsd = 1e-8
    while time.time() < stop:
        act = np.nonzero(c >= cur - 1e-9)[0]
        nz = act[act != p]
        has = nz.size > 0
        bf = cur
        bi = -1
        bj = -1
        blo = 0.0
        bhi = 0.0
        bd = 0.0
        tb = time.time()
        while time.time() - tb < 0.22 and time.time() < stop:
            i = rng.randint(n)
            j = rng.randint(n)
            if i == j:
                continue
            lo = max(h[i] - 1.0, -h[j])
            hi = min(h[i], 1.0 - h[j])
            if hi - lo < 1e-9:
                continue
            fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hr, i, j, n)
            if has:
                av = A[nz]
                bp = bool(np.any(av > epsd))
                bn = bool(np.any(av < -epsd))
            else:
                bp = False
                bn = False
            if hi > 1e-9 and lo < -1e-9 and not bp and not bn:
                a, b = lo, hi
            elif hi > 1e-9 and not bp:
                a, b = 0.0, hi
            elif lo < -1e-9 and not bn:
                a, b = lo, 0.0
            else:
                continue
            fv, dv = pair_search_interval(c, A, i, j, n, a, b, pts)
            if fv < bf - 1e-12:
                bf = fv
                bi = i
                bj = j
                blo = a
                bhi = b
                bd = dv
        if bi >= 0 and bf < cur - 1e-9:
            fill_grad_diff(A, omh, hr, bi, bj, n)
            dlt = refine_pair_delta(c, A, bi, bj, n, blo, bhi, bd)
            fv = float(pair_eval_f(c, A, bi, bj, n, np.array([dlt]))[0])
            if fv < cur - 1e-9:
                old = cur
                h[bi] -= dlt
                h[bj] += dlt
                h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                if abs(h.sum() - target) > 1e-10:
                    h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c = compute_counts(h)
                cur = float(c.max())
                omh = 1.0 - h
                hr = h[::-1]
                if cur < old - 1e-12:
                    no = 0
                    if cur < best_r:
                        best_r = cur
                        best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    no += 1
            else:
                no += 1
        else:
            no += 1
        if no >= 30:
            h = best_h.copy()
            h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, sig, size=n), n)
            c = compute_counts(h)
            cur = float(c.max())
            omh = 1.0 - h
            hr = h[::-1]
            no = 0
            sig = max(0.02, sig * 0.85)
    return best_h, best_r


def make_bit_masks(n):
    allm = (1 << n) - 1
    pv = []
    nv = []
    for d in range(1, n):
        pv.append((1 << (n - d)) - 1)
        nv.append(allm ^ ((1 << d) - 1))
    return pv, nv


def bit_score(mask, n, pos, neg):
    mx = 0
    cnt = 0
    ssq = 0
    for d in range(1, n):
        c = (mask & ~(mask >> d) & pos[d - 1]).bit_count()
        if c > mx:
            mx = c
            cnt = 1
        elif c == mx:
            cnt += 1
        ssq += c * c
        c = (mask & ~(mask >> d) & neg[d - 1]).bit_count()
        if c > mx:
            mx = c
            cnt = 1
        elif c == mx:
            cnt += 1
        ssq += c * c
    return mx, cnt, ssq


def bit_energy(s):
    return 100.0 * s[0] + 10.0 * s[1] + 0.001 * s[2]


def h_to_mask(h, n):
    m = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if h[i] >= 0.5:
            m |= (1 << i)
    return m


def mask_to_h(mask, n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        if (mask >> i) & 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    return h


def top_half_binary(h, n):
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
    out[idx[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return out


def random_balanced_mask(n, rng):
    v = np.ones(n // 2, dtype=float)
    v = np.concatenate((v, np.zeros(n - n // 2, dtype=float)))
    rng.shuffle(v)
    return h_to_mask(v, n)


def binary_sa(mask, n, stop, seed, pos, neg):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    cur = bit_score(mask, n, pos, neg)
    E = bit_energy(cur)
    bcur = cur
    bm = mask
    bE = E
    t0 = time.time()
    T0 = 80.0
    Te = 1.0
    den = max(1.0, stop - t0)
    stag = 0
    while time.time() < stop:
        while True:
            i = rng.randint(n)
            if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0:
                break
        while True:
            j = rng.randint(n)
            if ((mask >> j) & 1) != 0:
                break
        nm = mask ^ (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
        nc = bit_score(nm, n, pos, neg)
        nE = bit_energy(nc)
        acc = nE < E - 1e-9
        if not acc:
            frac = max(0.0, 1.0 - (time.time() - t0) / den)
            T = Te + (T0 - Te) * frac
            if T > 1e-3 and nE >= E:
                if rng.random() < math.exp(-(nE - E) / T):
                    acc = True
        if acc:
            mask = nm
            cur = nc
            E = nE
            stag = 0
            if E < bE - 1e-9:
                bE = E
                bcur = cur
                bm = mask
        else:
            stag += 1
        if stag > 6000:
            for _ in range(3):
                while True:
                    i = rng.randint(n)
                    if ((mask >> i) & 1) == 0:
                        break
                while True:
                    j = rng.randint(n)
                    if ((mask >> j) & 1) != 0:
                        break
                mask ^= (1 << i) ^ (1 << j)
            cur = bit_score(mask, n, pos, neg)
            E = bit_energy(cur)
            stag = 0
    return bm, bcur


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    dl = t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - 10.0)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h0)
    except Exception:
        n = 250
        h0 = np.full(n, 0.5)
    if n <= 0:
        n = 250
        h0 = mask_to_h(random_balanced_mask(n, np.random.RandomState(seed)), n)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_r = float(compute_counts(h0).max())
    total = max(1.0, dl - t0)
    bin_h = None

    if total > 120:
        stop1 = t0 + max(60.0, 0.20 * total)
        h1, r1 = pair_polish(h0, n, stop1, seed)
        if r1 < best_r:
            best_r = r1
            best_h = h1

    if (n % 2 == 0) and n < 600 and time.time() < dl - 120:
        stop2 = t0 + 0.40 * total
        if stop2 < time.time():
            stop2 = time.time()
        hbin = top_half_binary(best_h, n)
        m = h_to_mask(hbin, n)
        pos, neg = make_bit_masks(n)
        bm, sc = binary_sa(m, n, stop2, seed + 1, pos, neg)
        bin_h = mask_to_h(bm, n)
        if time.time() < dl - 120:
            stop3 = t0 + 0.65 * total
            hb, rb = pair_polish(bin_h, n, stop3, seed + 2)
            if rb < best_r:
                best_r = rb
                best_h = hb
            bin_h = hb

    starts = [best_h]
    if bin_h is not None:
        br = float(compute_counts(bin_h).max())
        if br < best_r + 2.0:
            starts.append(bin_h)

    end = dl - 3.0
    for idx, sh in enumerate(starts):
        rem = end - time.time()
        if rem < 30:
            break
        if len(starts) > 1 and idx == 0:
            seg_stop = time.time() + 0.55 * rem
        else:
            seg_stop = time.time() + rem
        hf, rf = pair_polish(sh, n, seg_stop, seed + 20 + idx)
        if rf < best_r:
            best_r = rf
            best_h = hf

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_r = float(compute_counts(best_h).max())
    return best_h, best_r * 2.0 / float(n), n
```
